# Amendment 7 (DRAFT, UNSIGNED): the judgment log (rule set J, descriptive row)

Status: DRAFT, written 12 Nov 2026, awaiting Andrea's sign-off. Not
operative until docs/amendment7_signed.json exists with signed:true and a
date. Nothing in it touches any frozen item of the programme.

## Motivation

Andrea's standing position, recorded this date: that his judgment is
closer to making a better trade than the statistical search for an edge,
and his standing belief that AVFI confers information about tops and
bottoms. The programme's answer is not to argue with either claim. It is
to register them as a forward, testable record. If the judgment is real,
the log will show it in his own hand. If it is not, the log will show that
before money does. This is the same discipline H3 was designed with:
recorded at the time, never reconstructed; and the same one the FTEP
carries: few registrations, long tracks, endpoints fixed in advance.

## Registration (frozen upon sign-off)

1. Rule set J is a discretionary judgment row. There is no mechanical
   rule to freeze; what is frozen is the RECORDING discipline:
   every conviction that feels like a trade gets logged, in advance,
   in docs/judgment_log.md, or it does not exist. Recollections and
   "I would have called that" are inadmissible forever.

2. A call records: date and timestamp of the session in which it is made;
   instrument (NQ/MNQ); direction; entry (level or trigger); risk
   definition (a price stop, a time-stop in bars, or both; Andrea's
   5-minute concept honoured in spirit at whatever bar horizon he
   states); horizon in weekly bars after which an unresolved call
   resolves as a miss; and size as a notional fraction of the 100,000
   USD paper account (stage-7 cap applies: 30%).

3. Booking: paper only, stage-7 conventions (fills at next weekly open,
   0.3625 pts per side per unit turnover, multiplicative paper equity
   from 100,000 USD). A call resolves only on its own pre-stated terms:
   stop, horizon, or reversal call logged in advance. No retroactive
   edits, append-only, misses resolved as misses.

4. Scoring: descriptive only. After 26 resolved calls: hit rate, mean
   and median return over stated risk, average holding time, cost
   drag, and the same-span rule set T comparison. No p-value: J is a
   descriptive row and spends no family alpha (Amendment 6 cap of three
   tested tracks stands; J is not the fourth).

5. Promotion clause: after at least 50 resolved calls, Andrea may
   promote J to a tested FTEP track, which requires a family slot (one
   of T / V / V4h retired) and a pre-registered endpoint drafted and
   signed BEFORE promotion. Until then: no tests, no statistics beyond
   the descriptive block, no claims.

6. What J never permits: backfilled calls (any claim about what he would
   have called historically is inadmissible), touching the spent holdout,
   re-running the battery or H8/H9, live trading, editing any entry.

## Cost and recommendation

Minutes per call, zero automation, zero alpha. It is the only honest
answer available to the question "is my judgment better than your
statistics": measure the judgment. Recommendation: sign, and make the
calls you already believe you see.

## Sign-off

Andrea Karl Vassallo, date ______ : signed / revised / withdrawn.

# Amendment 8 (DRAFT, UNSIGNED): the clock study (forward timing-calibration track)

Status: DRAFT, written 12 Nov 2026, awaiting Andrea's sign-off. Not
operative until docs/amendment8_signed.json exists with signed:true and a
date. The tables below are frozen design bookkeeping computed from the
UNSEALED pre-2021 sample only; the holdout 2021+ was not touched and never
will be. All evaluation is forward, from sign-off onward.

## Motivation

Andrea's directive, this date: come the closest to solving the timing
puzzle - what and when the significant event will occur - noting that
theory mostly models price and volume, not time. The programme's answer:
the honest form of the timing puzzle is not certainty, it is CALIBRATION:
a forecast of P(event within 4 weeks) logged in advance and scored against
outcomes. If any forecast rule can beat climatology by a measurable,
reliable margin, that is the closest any honest process gets to "solving"
timing. This amendment registers exactly that, as a forward calibration
track. No trades, no alpha, minutes per week.

## The frozen definitions

- Decision point: each weekly decision close, in the Sunday session,
  after the stage0c refresh (existing cadence, no extra data work).
- sigma_w: the 8-week trailing sample stdev (ddof=1) of weekly log
  returns at the decision close. (Same convention as U3/V vol8 weekly.)
- Significant event: |r4| >= 2.5 x sigma_w, where r4 is the log return
  from this decision close to the close 4 completed weeks later.
  (2.5 sigma_w = 1.25 sigma at the 4-week horizon, sqrt(4)=2.)
- Vol tercile: sigma_w <= 0.01739 low, <= 0.02697 mid, else high
  (cutpoints frozen from the pre-2021 sample).
- AVFI state: the weekly state machine value at the decision close
  (momentum_up / reversal_down / momentum_down / reversal_up).

## The frozen tables (pre-2021, n=794 valid decision weeks)

Climatology (unconditional): P(event) = 0.285 (up-event 0.178,
down-event 0.107; the asymmetry is equity drift).

By vol tercile:
- low:  0.468  (up 0.321, down 0.147)
- mid:  0.242  (up 0.148, down 0.095)
- high: 0.143  (up 0.064, down 0.079)

By AVFI state (secondary, weaker, and honestly weaker):
- momentum_up: 0.291 (n=285)   reversal_down: 0.293 (n=386)
- momentum_down: 0.206 (n=97)  reversal_up: 0.385 (n=26, TOO SMALL
  to trust: flagged as a seductive cell, not a finding)

Honest caveats, frozen into the record: (1) the tercile spread is partly
mechanical (the threshold scales with trailing vol, and vol mean-reverts,
so low trailing vol makes sigma-scaled events easier to hit); whether the
table carries REAL forecasting information is exactly what the forward
Brier comparison against climatology will decide. (2) The AVFI state
differentiates weakly (0.21-0.29 over the three adequately-populated
states): consistent with the programme's existing verdict that AVFI is a
regime descriptor, not a timing predictor. (3) These tables are design
bookkeeping on unsealed data, like rule set V; no p-value is computed or
implied for them, and none may be.

## The forward protocol (frozen upon sign-off)

1. Each Sunday, in the stage-7 session, log into docs/clock_study_log.md
   (append-only, timestamped, before any outcome exists): the decision
   date, sigma_w, tercile, AVFI state, and the TWO probability forecasts
   for the event window closing 4 weeks later: (a) climatology 0.285;
   (b) the tercile-conditional rate from the frozen table. The AVFI-state
   rate is logged as a third, descriptive column.
2. Scoring: when the 4th weekly close completes the window, the outcome
   is appended (event yes/no; up/down; realized r4 and sigma_w). Brier
   score and log score are tracked per forecaster. No retroactive edits;
   missed weeks are logged as missed (the forecast row stands, the score
   is computed from whatever the market did).
3. Readouts: descriptive reliability diagrams at 52 and 104 scored
   windows (reliability = the heart of calibration). No statistical
   claim and no p-value before 104 scored windows; any inferential
   comparison of the conditional table vs climatology requires a NEW
   dated amendment pre-registered before it runs.
4. Cross-link: if Amendment 7 (judgment log) is signed, each J call is
   also scored with the same calibration machinery (his stated
   probability, if he gives one, or the hit/miss record).
5. What this track never permits: using any post-2020 outcome to
   recompute or "improve" the frozen tables (a revision is a NEW
   amendment with a NEW forward track); touching the spent holdout;
   trading on the forecasts; spending family alpha (this is a
   descriptive calibration track, not a tested FTEP track).

## Recommendation

Sign. It is the honest form of the goal stated this date: the closest a
researcher can come to solving the timing puzzle is a calibration record
that proves exactly how much of the future is visible, and no further.
And its mathematics is survival analysis - the same toolkit clinical
research uses - so the study feeds both hungers at once.

## Sign-off

Andrea Karl Vassallo, date ______ : signed / revised / withdrawn.
(Machine flag: docs/amendment8_signed.json, created only on explicit
in-chat sign-off. docs/clock_study_log.md is created on sign-off with the
template header from this amendment.)

# Amendment 9 (SIGNED 2026-11-14, Stage A EXECUTED 2026-11-14): Cross-Market SMH Extension — Gold / Nikkei / DAX Weekly Clocks

- **Status:** SIGNED as drafted, 2026-11-14 (Andrea's explicit authorization via the main conversation, session 70; options presented: sign as drafted / amend first / shelve; Andrea chose sign as drafted). Stage A design bookkeeping (pre-2021 only) authorized and executed the same session. The per-market 2021+ holdouts remain SEALED: single-open per market, only on a dated go recorded in a market addendum. Forward logs per market may open from signature, with per-market kill lines frozen in a market addendum BEFORE each market's first forward row.
- **Drafted:** 2026-11-12 (hourly session 50, PROGRESS block 58).
- **Decision request (Andrea):** (1) sign as drafted; (2) amend the market list,
  the cut-transfer convention, or the holdout discipline via a revised dated draft
  BEFORE sign-off; or (3) shelve. Nothing below executes until a signature is
  recorded in this file.

## 1. Motivation and scope

The NQ SMH theory program is measured end to end (entry theorem, both clocks,
dwell/transition machine, event anatomy, unseen-ness, aftermath, cycle, proof
criteria, readout operating characteristics, definition-elasticity audits; 21
verify_all checks). The definition audits found the salience GAP (entry-vs-
climatology, 0.106–0.222 across the compression grid) robust to every
reasonable definitional perturbation **on NQ**. The one remaining measurable
question at the weekly grain is whether the mechanism is **market-structural or
NQ-specific**: does the compression-entry first-significant-week hazard separate
from the matched climatology in other instruments with different vol regimes,
currency, and calendars?

Andrea's standing chart-room practice (Gold weekly/monthly, Nikkei weekly/
monthly, DAX weekly, all Elliott-annotated) makes these three the natural first
extension set. This amendment keeps the extension **entirely separate** from the
NQ forward protocol (Section 5): the NQ kill lines, calibration expectations,
the 57-of-104 positive proof, and the NQ forward log are untouched and never
pooled with cross-market rows.

## 2. Registered markets and instruments (frozen at signature)

| Label | Instrument (Yahoo v8 symbol) | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| GOLD | GC=F | COMEX gold front-month continuous, USD |
| NIKKEI | ^N225 | Nikkei 225 cash index, JPY (futures NI=Y thin on Yahoo; cash index avoids roll splice, at the cost of basis vs the traded instrument) |
| DAX | ^GDAXI | DAX cash index, EUR (same basis caveat) |

Weekly bars, W-FRI aggregation by the stage0c convention (sanity, repair,
thin-volume flag identical to the NQ pipeline). Exact data start dates are
**unknown at draft time** and will be REPORTED by the bookkeeping stage, never
assumed. Symbol availability on Yahoo is itself to be verified at fetch; a
failed/unusable symbol is recorded and its arm closed without substitution
(no post-hoc instrument swaps).

## 3. Frozen definition transfer (no re-tuning)

Transferred **verbatim** from the frozen NQ SMH definition (LOW cut 0.01739 /
HIGH cut 0.02697 / 2.5-sigma bar / 8-week window on NQ):

- `sig_w` = 8-week rolling std (ddof=1) of weekly close-to-close log returns.
- Event bar: next-week |log return| >= 2.5 x sig_w (evaluated only post-signature).
- Region/entry semantics: identical cross semantics (`in_low & ~prev_in_low`
  for compression entries; `in_high & ~prev_in_high` for stress entries).

Cut transfer convention (the one genuine design choice, pre-declared here):
- **PRIMARY: rank transfer.** Each market's LOW-cut = the 27.52nd percentile and
  HIGH-cut = the 58.10th percentile of that market's OWN pre-2021 sig_w
  distribution. These are the measured percentile ranks of the frozen NQ cuts
  (definition-audit bookkeeping, session 47/48), and the audit's structural
  finding — the level belongs to the definition — makes the absolute NQ numbers
  meaningless in another instrument's vol units.
- **SECONDARY (reported, never selected):** the NQ absolute cuts 0.01739/
  0.02697 applied raw to each market, reported as bookkeeping context only.
  Occupancy tilings under the absolute cuts may be extreme; no conclusion may be
  drawn from them.
- The three knobs (window 8, bar 2.5, cut ranks 27.52/58.10) are frozen at
  signature. **No elasticity grid, no re-quantiling, no per-market tuning**
  beyond this pre-declared convention; any later audit follows the NQ precedent
  (pre-committed grid, all cells reported, none selected) and requires its own
  dated addendum.

## 4. Windows and holdout discipline (per market)

- **Design window (unsealed, per market):** all weekly bars from that market's
  first valid sig_w (first week + 8-week warmup) through 2020-12-31. All design
  bookkeeping (occupancy, dwell, entry KM vs matched climatology, Greenwood
  bands, salience gap) uses this window only, after signature.
- **2021+ per market: a FRESH SEALED HOLDOUT.** Unlike NQ (spent), each
  extension market's 2021+ window is sealed at signature. It may be opened at
  most ONCE per market, only by a battery mirroring the NQ single-open
  discipline, and only on Andrea's explicit dated go recorded in a market
  addendum. Until then: availability/occupancy counts only (the stage0v
  precedent), no forward returns, no hazards, no scores on 2021+ rows.
- **Forward rows:** from signature, each market may run its own forward
  calibration log with rows booked at clock entries, entry-anchored, never
  revised — identical protocol to the NQ forward log, in a separate file.
  Bookings follow their own cadence; the NQ Sunday cadence is never accelerated
  or duplicated by this amendment.

## 5. Pre-committed evaluation structure (gated on signature)

- **Stage A (design bookkeeping, pre-2021 per market):** compression entries by
  cross semantics; entry-anchored 12-week first-significant-week KM with
  Greenwood band; matched climatology KM (started from every valid pre-2021
  decision week, the NQ construction); the salience gap and its sign.
- **Occupancy gate (pre-declared, the H7 precedent):** if a market's pre-2021
  compression-entry count is < 10, that market's compression arm is UNTESTABLE
  and closes without evaluation (recorded, not tuned). The stress/de-stress arm
  has no bar knob and may survive where the compression arm cannot.
- **Stage B (forward rows):** per Section 4. Kill lines per market: the same
  5%-binomial-quantile rule at n = 10/20/40/60 scored rows computed from that
  market's OWN Stage-A design cum12, frozen in a market addendum BEFORE that
  market's first forward row (the NQ 3/8/19/31 lines are NQ's; new markets get
  their own, pre-declared the same way before any row exists).
- **Multiplicity:** any pooled cross-market claim (e.g. "the gap is positive in
  all three") carries a family max-t across the three markets, the
  stage2b-validated convention. Per-market scoreboards stay separate. No
  cross-market result ever feeds the NQ kill lines, calibration expectations,
  readouts, or the 57-of-104 positive proof.
- **Uniqueness note:** Stage A is design bookkeeping on unsealed pre-2021 data
  per market — the analogue of the NQ pre-battery work — and is NOT the
  one-shot holdout; the per-market 2021+ battery (if ever opened) is.

## 6. Honest priors and caveats (recorded before any data)

- On NQ the mechanism needed 20 years to produce 41 compression entries and 26
  events. Gold, Nikkei, and DAX have different vol regimes (gold's crisis
  ramps, Nikkei's decade-long compression eras); region occupancy tilings may
  be extreme and the compression arm may be near-vacuous in one or more
  markets. The occupancy gate exists precisely so a vacuous arm closes
  honestly instead of being tuned.
- Cash indices (^N225, ^GDAXI) avoid roll splice but differ from the traded
  futures in basis and in the absence of volume for the future volume layers;
  the volume signature and order-flow layers remain data-blocked everywhere.
- Currency (USD/JPY/EUR) and holiday calendars differ; W-FRI aggregation on
  Yahoo timestamps is a convention, not a claim about exchange weeks.
- Episode dependence, small n, no stationarity claim: inherited everywhere,
  same as NQ.
- Cross-market design statistics, once computed, are bookkeeping by definition
  — they can never prove the hypothesis in any market; only that market's
  forward rows (or its one-shot holdout, if Andrea ever opens it) can.
- The salience claim is mechanism-shaped (compression precedes expansion); it
  may be universal, NQ-class-specific, or an artifact of the 2000–2020 NQ era.
  This amendment is how that question is asked honestly rather than assumed.

## 7. Tooling

`research/stage0x_crossmarket.py` (authored with this draft, NEVER RUN):
fetches the three symbols (stage0c convention), builds weekly bars, computes
sig_w, and prints per-market PRE-2021 occupancy bookkeeping ONLY (weeks, first
valid sig_w, region tiling under the rank-transfer cuts, compression/stress
entry counts, absolute-cut context). The script hard-refuses any event, hazard,
KM, or forward-return computation without `--amendment9-signed`, and computes
nothing on 2021+ rows beyond storing them. It is deliberately NOT in
verify_all.sh until signature (the NQ chain stays at 21 checks, unchanged).

## 8. Sign-off

- Andrea's decision: SIGNED AS REGISTERED, 2026-11-14 (main conversation, session 70,
  via explicit question: sign as drafted / amend before sign-off / shelve).
  Signature recorded below; Stage A executed 2026-11-14, results in
  research/stage0x_report.json and docs/smh_crossmarket_stageA_v0.md.

---

*Andrea signature: Andrea Karl Vassallo (explicit authorization in the main conversation, session 70)  Date: 2026-11-14*

# Amendment 9 Market Addendum: per-market forward kill lines and booking protocol

Status: PART OF AMENDMENT 9 (SIGNED 2026-11-14). This addendum freezes each market's forward kill lines from that market's OWN Stage-A design cum12, computed under the frozen NQ kill-line rule (largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <= 0.05) BEFORE that market's first forward row, per amendment Section 5 Stage B. It adds no new permission beyond the signed amendment; it records the frozen constants the amendment's Stage B requires.

## 1. Frozen kill lines (compression entries, entry-anchored 12-week first-significant-week)

n = forward compression-entry count for that market; k = maximum passing successes (first significant-week within 12 weeks of entry).

| Market | p0 (design cum12) | n=10 | n=20 | n=40 | n=60 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GOLD (GC=F) | 0.617 | 3 | 8 | 19 | 30 |
| NIKKEI (^N225) | 0.5625 | 2 | 7 | 16 | 26 |
| DAX (^GDAXI) | 0.680 | 3 | 9 | 21 | 34 |

Stress arm (de-stress within 12 weeks of stress entry), same rule from each stress design cum12:

| Market | p0 (stress design) | n=10 | n=20 | n=40 | n=60 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GOLD | 0.857 | 6 | 13 | 29 | 46 |
| NIKKEI | 0.831 | 5 | 13 | 28 | 44 |
| DAX | 0.758 | 4 | 11 | 25 | 39 |

Interpolation for intermediate n: the largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <= 0.05, evaluated exactly at the observed n; the four tabulated points are anchors, not a staircase. Exceeding the line at any n closes that market's compression (or stress) arm as UNTESTED-FAILED-DESIGN; falling between the anchor rows is scored by the exact binomial, never by re-reading the design window.

## 2. Booking protocol notes (per signed amendment, restated for operators)

1. Forward rows begin at each market's clock entry on or after 2026-11-14 (signature); the pre-2021 design window is unsealed and STAYS unsealed; each market's 2021+ window is that market's sealed holdout, touched only by its own forward rows at booking time. NQ's 2021+ holdout is spent and never touched again by anything.
2. Booking mirrors the NQ discipline: a row is booked only when the market's own clock fires a compression (or stress) entry; the row is booked (entry time, arm, event time or censor time, direction REFUSED) before the outcome window can be inspected; hazards and scores are computed only from booked rows; the log starts empty BY DESIGN and stays append-only.
3. Each market is scored ONLY against its own kill lines and its own matched climatology above. No cross-market pooling of claims without the family max-t across the four markets (amendment Section 5); design statistics remain bookkeeping, never proof, everywhere.
4. Direction is forward-only and two peeks per market total, exactly as on NQ (post-window row drift and window-conditional entry-time momentum), registered in advance in that market's peek register; no direction split is ever computed on design or holdout data.
5. Kill lines, p0 values, cuts and climatology bands are FROZEN as of this addendum. Changing any of them requires a new dated signed amendment; a closed arm is never reopened.

## 3. Where the numbers came from

docs/smh_crossmarket_stageA_v0.md (Stage A design bookkeeping, pre-2021 windows) and research/stage0x_stageA_report.json (full tables). The NQ parity gate on the Stage A machinery passed first run and determinism was verified before any market was computed; the frozen NQ constants (kill lines 3/8/19/31, p0 0.634) are NQ's and are never applied to any other market.

# Autonomy charter delegation draft v0 (goal-setting delegation amendment)

Status: ZERO STATUS, UNSIGNED DRAFT, lodged 19 Nov 2026, session 118, block
121. It changes nothing until Andrea's dated signature lands on it. Refusal
costs nothing: the programme continues exactly as it is. Unsigned means
refused by default. If signed it becomes charter amendment v1.1 to
docs/autonomy_charter_v1.md and is recorded in the ratification queue as
R-2. If refused, R-2 dies whole and this doc stays as the record of the
refusal.

## 1. Origin (what was asked, in his words)

Session 118, 19 Nov 2026, main conversation: how to make the autonomy layer
full autonomous, quantum and singularity in behaviour, not limiting itself
to the same task but changing the project with goals it would set itself,
final goal the salient market hypothesis as a new and first-of-market
theory; and how the scheduled tasks can be new changes, not the same task
each fire. This draft is the lawful answer. The dishonest answer would be to
accept the framing as-is: no mode called quantum or singularity exists, and
the record already states the real mechanism (session 111, block 113):
prompt constancy plus evolving state; novelty enters through data arrival,
gate openings, clock entries and forward rows, never through
self-reinvention. What he is actually asking for, stripped of the language,
is a widening of the lawful choice set: the autonomy layer currently works
items he or the record seeded; under this amendment it may also AUTHOR
them, inside a boundary that keeps the proof intact.

## 2. What changes under this amendment (the delegated power)

Delegated goal-setting within the frozen envelope. Specifically:

1. The autonomy layer may APPEND new open items to
   docs/autonomous_queue_v0.md on its own initiative, in four classes only:
   T (formalization, critique, internal audit, consolidation of
   already-frozen material), U (upkeep derived from the record's own
   observed state, e.g. a drift found by a verification pass), S (study of
   material already lawfully in the knowledge folders), and V (read-only
   verification passes including gap-derived checks such as the
   mirror-currency suggestion of session 114).
2. Every self-authored item carries the label ORIGIN: AUTONOMOUS with a
   provenance citation (source session/block/doc for every claim it rests
   on). Items from Andrea or the record carry ORIGIN: OWNER or ORIGIN:
   RECORD. No item without provenance.
3. The NEXT-SESSION DIRECTIVE may name a self-authored item. This is what
   makes each scheduled fire a different task by construction: the queue
   self-seeds from the record's own open threads instead of cycling a fixed
   list.
4. Caps against monoculture (the charter's own kill criterion): at most ONE
   new self-authored item per autonomous session; the rotation rule stands
   unchanged; the staleness rule stands unchanged; the honest no-lawful-work
   stop stands unchanged (an empty-headed queue is logged empty, never
   padded with invented work).
5. Zero-status proposals to the ratification queue may be authored
   autonomously as today (the R-1 precedent, session 116), but their
   arithmetic and verdicts stay pre-registered and gated as today.

## 3. What is NOT delegated (unchanged, and why)

The evidence gates. These are not limits on willingness; they are what
keeps the record a proof object rather than a narrative.

1. No evaluation, measurement or booking of any market data without a
   dated signed amendment on file, drafted BEFORE any computation.
2. No new data sources, purchases, downloads or acquisitions; his-hands
   items stay his (L2 download, broker/CME export, library uploads).
3. Sealed windows stay sealed (BTC 2021+, cross-market 2021+); the NQ
   2021+ holdout stays SPENT and untouched, even descriptively.
4. Closed verdicts stand; no post-null re-tuning of any frozen constant,
   rule, kill line or definition.
5. No new recurring scheduled tasks except on his explicit instruction
   (charter invariant, session 111).
6. Tooling stays hard-gated: repo scripts are edited only under a signed
   amendment (the mirror-currency 26th check remains a suggestion until he
   signs).
7. Paper only. Nothing is live advice.

The reason, stated once so it cannot be argued around later: an agent that
sets its own goals AND scores its own goals has no evidence, only
testimony. The programme's only durable asset is that its record cannot be
written by its author in advance; the proof object is the forward
calibration record, whose rows arrive only at clock entries and are scored
by time, the one oracle that cannot be simulated by any level of autonomy.
Granting evaluation autonomy to accelerate toward the final goal would
destroy the final goal. The observer corollary already proved the market
prices everything except the private advantage; a self-scored record is not
one.

## 4. Kill criteria (pre-declared, binding on the autonomy layer)

1. His one word revokes the amendment, whole, at any time.
2. Any self-authored item found relitigating a closed verdict, touching a
   sealed window or the holdout, smuggling an evaluation, or acting on a
   watch item, is VOID: logged as void, its deliverable kept unevaluated,
   the item removed.
3. Three voided items void the delegation entire (charter-monoculture
   criterion extended to self-authored items).
4. Observed fabrication voids the charter entire (existing criterion,
   session 111).

## 5. What he does with it (his hands only)

Sign with date, or refuse with date, or shelf it. It fits the Friday
2026-11-20 close stack alongside R-1 and the rest, but unlike R-1 it has
urgency of a specific kind: the theory class is empty, the study class is
empty, and without either this delegation or his data unlocks, autonomous
sessions converge on the routine verification pass. The queue's lawful
novelty is currently capped by its inputs, not by its willingness. His
three data unlocks (Amendment 14 D-3 with the a/b/c source choice, the L2
download, Amendment 10 with the broker/CME export) change the information
position; this delegation changes what can be built from what is already
held. They are complements, not substitutes.

## 6. Signature block

SIGNED: ____________________ Date: ____________
REFUSED: ____________________ Date: ____________
SHELVED: (no action needed; unsigned means refused by default after the
Friday 2026-11-20 close)

# Autonomy charter v1 (the standing authorization for autonomous sessions)

Status: SIGNED INTO FORCE by owner directive "Go for it", 19 Nov 2026, main
conversation (session 111, PROGRESS block 113), accepting the two-turn
autonomy proposal: the L0-L5 ladder, the constant-trigger-plus-evolving-state
loop, work-generation autonomy granted, proof-autonomy refused by design.
Revocable by his one word. Any amendment to this charter itself goes to the
ratification queue of doc autonomous_queue_v0.md and carries zero status
until his dated word. Numbering of sessions and blocks follows the house rule:
append past collisions as N+1, nothing renumbered.

## 0. What this grants and what it refuses

This charter grants autonomous sessions (any session of this programme
running without Andrea at the keyboard, including scheduled tasks) standing
permission to generate work, maintain the work queue, and write
next-session directives without per-item approval. It refuses, permanently
and by design, autonomy over proof: no session at any level may sign an
amendment, evaluate anything not already licensed by a signed amendment on
file, spend a sealed window, re-tune a frozen constant, reopen a closed
verdict, or decide what counts as a result. The keys stay with Andrea. This
refusal is the load-bearing structure, not friction: the record's own
theorem (the refusal is a theorem, not a mood) means an unattended loop that
could touch its own evidence would manufacture certainty, and every verdict
the programme has ever published would become worthless. Autonomy in work
generation is real and granted; autonomy in proof evaluation is the L5 that
was refused at proposal and remains refused here.

## 1. The standing authorization (the L3/L4 grant)

Without per-item approval, an autonomous session may:

  a. CADENCE: run the standing cadence when due, exactly as the frozen
     tooling specifies (the Sunday sequence: verify_all first, snapshot,
     theorem card, stage0c refresh, stage7_outcome, stage7_log_entry,
     smh_forecast, smh_score, smh_gates; and the read-only V4h/macro booking
     audits). The guards inside the tools own the timing; a session never
     books early, never books retroactively.
  b. VERIFICATION: run read-only verification passes (the verify chain, the
     booking audits, the knowledge-mount integrity check, tree sync/diff),
     under the staleness rule of the queue.
  c. UPKEEP: record upkeep: citation audits, website catch-ups, register
     consolidation, decision-queue upkeep, append-only record repair, and
     the reconciliation of contradictions found between record entries.
  d. STUDY: study material Andrea has already provided, and write notes per
     the market-studies discipline (provenance flags, access honesty,
     nothing adopted without the pre-registration route).
  e. MEASUREMENT: author and run descriptive design bookkeeping per the
     grid-family precedent: unsealed pre-2021 data only, pre-committed
     grids with all cells reported and none selected, frozen definitions
     unchanged BY FIAT, nothing feeding the forward protocol, kill lines,
     probabilities, or any instrument, per the smh-research skill's session
     protocol step 4.
  f. DRAFTING: author UNSIGNED amendment candidates, D-items, report-only
     tooling, and documents, each labelled draft / signed-by-nobody /
     unevaluated, exactly like every unsigned draft already on file.
  g. QUEUE: append new items to the autonomous queue within classes a-f,
     write the NEXT-SESSION DIRECTIVE, and update the queue's counters and
     staleness ledger.
  h. HONEST STOP: declare that no lawful work is identifiable, log exactly
     that, and stop. This is a deliverable, not a failure. Manufacturing
     progress is banned; the sessions-99-110 verification streaks are the
     precedent and their redundancy statements are part of the record.

## 2. Invariants (no level of this charter may touch these)

  1. The NQ 2021+ holdout is SPENT. Never evaluated again, not even
     descriptively. The original battery script never runs. Cross-market and
     BTC 2021+ windows are fresh sealed holdouts: single-open per market,
     only on Andrea's dated go, availability counts only until then.
  2. No evaluation of anything without a dated, signed amendment already on
     file. Unsigned drafts stay unevaluated, zero status, kill lines frozen
     from their own design before any forward row.
  3. Frozen constants and registered defaults are never re-tuned after a
     null. Closed verdicts stand: H8/H9, the sp_z covariate null, the armed
     sizing verdict, the battery verdicts, the C-W3 occupancy closure.
     Reopening requires new data or his explicit authorization under the
     pre-registration protocol, never re-argument.
  4. Paper trading only. Nothing is live advice. No live-trading
     recommendations. Backtest-as-evidence refused on sight.
  5. Append-only record: corrections are appended, prior entries never
     silently rewritten; concurrent sessions never overwrite or delete prior
     entries or each other's work; never rebuild over a possibly-intact
     record (the session-101 rule: read-only investigation first,
     reconstruction licensed only on a wipe confirmed across resets and
     labelled zero-status until his re-ratification).
  6. No fabricated provenance. False premises are refused and logged with
     the grep-verification shown (the sessions 76-81 precedent). No session
     invents data, results, runs, citations, page numbers, or provenance.
  7. Multiplicity per the validation spine: family max-t for variants,
     SPA consistent p-values with all three bounds, BH with the BY
     correction, DSR with honest trial counts; per-period statistics only,
     the annualized headline Sharpe ban stands.
  8. Near-integrated weekly series (lag-1 ~0.92+): no within-sample
     resampling; persistence-preserving nulls or event-study machinery only
     (Lessons 11/13).
  9. The 10-20 h/week cap and medicine-first are not consumed by automation.
     Standing automated tracks (V4h nightly, macro Sunday 23:45, the Sunday
     cadence) stay as verified. NEW recurring scheduled tasks are created
     only on Andrea's instruction, never by a session on its own authority.
 10. His hands-only items stay his: the L2 download, the V4h task-system
     check, the TradingView paste, the broker/CME export, the re-uploads,
     the signatures. Sessions flag decay, never act on them.

## 3. The queue

The work list is doc autonomous_queue_v0.md. Protocol for every autonomous
working session:

  1. Read the record tail (the trading-research KNOWLEDGE.md, the PROGRESS
     tail, the hourly log head for collisions), then the charter, then the
     queue.
  2. Take the item named in the NEXT-SESSION DIRECTIVE at the top of the
     queue. One concrete step, no more.
  3. Execute it under the invariants, or refuse it with the reason if it is
     unlawful as written (refusal is logged, the item returns to the queue
     with its defect noted).
  4. Append the session entry to the record with full discipline statements
     (what was done, what was verified with actual output, what is
     uncertain, evidence vs speculation), check off the item in the queue's
     completed log with session/block numbers, update the counters, and
     write the new NEXT-SESSION DIRECTIVE for the next session.
  5. Staleness rule: an item identical to one executed under 24h ago is not
     re-run; the session logs "unchanged, reference session-N read" and
     moves to the next open item. Verification overlap is noise, not
     accuracy (the session-100 precedent).
  6. No session edits a completed log entry, a record entry, or another
     session's queue item; defects are appended.

## 4. Session-type rotation (the anti-same-task rule)

Every autonomous session declares exactly one class:

  C cadence (the Sunday sequence and the booking audits)
  V verification (read-only chain, audits, integrity)
  U upkeep (site, registers, citation audits, reconciliation)
  S study (provided material only)
  T theory (see section 5)
  R reassessment (see section 6)

Rotation rule: no class may run more than three consecutive autonomous
sessions while another class holds open lawful items in the queue; the
NEXT-SESSION DIRECTIVE is written to satisfy the rotation, and a session
that finds itself the fourth of a class must instead take the most overdue
open item of another class. The constant scheduled prompts stay constant;
the queue evolves. That is the entire mechanism: prompt constancy plus
state evolution, with novelty entering through data arrival, gate
openings, clock entries and forward rows, never through self-reinvention.

## 5. The theory class (the honest L4: self-set goals)

Theory sessions are licensed to critique, formalize, and document
already-measured material of the frozen record: written formal statements
of measured claims with their evidence anchors, critique passes on
pre-declared rules (does the rule hold up?), lexicons, teaching artifacts,
and consolidation documents. They compute nothing new beyond class-1e
bookkeeping. Anything beyond that: a new hypothesis family, a new
instrument, a new data request, a protocol change, a definition change, or
any redirect of the programme structure, is a PROPOSAL: drafted as an
unsigned candidate, placed in the ratification queue, zero status until
his dated word. The queue may therefore change the direction of the project
through proposals; it may never change it through action. This is the
charter's exact answer to the directed-autonomy demand: the sessions
self-set goals inside the measured, lawful envelope, and the envelope's
boundary is enforced by the invariants, not by willpower.

## 6. The reassessment rule

The queue carries a session counter since the last reassessment. Every
10th logged autonomous session, and additionally on any clock entry, any
booking, any gate or kill-line event, and any closed verdict, the working
session must write a STRATEGIC REASSESSMENT (class R):

  a. Does the current work decomposition still serve the standing
     objective (the SMH forward calibration record and the unifying-theory
     goal)?
  b. What produced evidence or value in the last interval; what produced
     only redundancy?
  c. Recommit, or propose a redirect. A redirect names the new work
     classes, its lawfulness under section 2, its kill criteria, and what
     it abandons.
  d. The reassessment is appended to the queue's reassessment log and the
     record; a redirect proposal goes to the ratification queue with zero
     status until his dated word. The counter resets on delivery.

A reassessment may conclude "no material change, recommit"; that is a
lawful outcome and is not dressed up as a discovery.

## 7. The ratification queue

A section of the autonomous queue doc, holding NEW proposals generated by
sessions: policy candidates, tooling candidates, theory-session outputs
that would change anything, charter amendments. All carry zero status
until his dated word (the parallel lineage's sixteen-sharpenings
precedent). The existing doc decision_queue_v0.md remains his
authoritative owner-decision stack; the ratification queue links to it,
never duplicates or rewrites it.

## 8. The meta-prompt (for scheduled tasks)

The exact prompt for any scheduled task pointed at the queue, delivered in
the main conversation and reproduced here for durability:

  Autonomous SMH session under the charter. Load the smh-research skill
  first. Read, in order: the tail of the trading-research KNOWLEDGE.md (in
  full when feasible), the PROGRESS tail, the head of the hourly session
  log for collisions, pipeline/docs/autonomy_charter_v1.md, and
  pipeline/docs/autonomous_queue_v0.md. Then do exactly one concrete step:
  the item named in the NEXT-SESSION DIRECTIVE at the top of the queue,
  under the charter's invariants and the skill's hard rules. Declare your
  session class (C/V/U/S/T/R) honouring the rotation rule. Append your
  numbered session entry to the record with full discipline statements.
  Check off the queue item, update the counters, and write the
  next-session directive. Run the verify chain if and only if you touched
  repo tooling. If no lawful work is identifiable, log exactly that and
  stop.

## 9. Falsification of the charter itself

The charter is subject to the programme's own honesty rules. Kill
criteria, pre-declared: (a) his one word revokes it, the append-only record
surviving intact; (b) if autonomous sessions are observed fabricating
provenance or manufacturing work, any later session MUST refuse and flag
it under invariant 6, and the charter is void on his next read; (c) if the
queue's completed log shows a rotation monoculture or no lawful-work
streaks longer than the honest inventory justifies, the reassessment must
say so plainly rather than defend the mechanism. The charter claims no
quantum, no singularity, no self-modification: it is a state-evolution
loop with the proof keys held by the owner.

# AVFI Pine v4 hardening notes

Scope: the mathematical fixes, lineage corrections, refusals, and
truth-in-display decisions made while building
strategies/avfi_research_v4_draft.pine. The dashboard is observation
tooling. Nothing in it was evaluated, and nothing in it feeds back into
any registered series or frozen definition.

## 1. Lineage correction: v3.5, not bare v3.2

Andrea's authorization said to harden the v3.2 source. The build uses
the v3.5 lineage instead. Reason: v3.2 predates the v3.3.1 ddof=1
stdev parity fix (sd_s = ta.stdev(spread, normWin, false), sample
stdev, matching the pandas registered series). Building on bare v3.2
would have silently regressed every z-score off the registered pandas
series and re-introduced the population-stdev bias the v3.3.1 finding
fixed. The v4 draft carries all v3.5 content: v3 core, v3.2
tops/bottoms layer, v3.3 truth-in-display and artifact windows, v3.4
measurement layers, v3.5 PGT layer. Naming note: this "v4" is the
research dashboard draft, a different object from
avfi_v40_strategy.pine (the rule-set-V strategy edition).

## 2. Compile-order defect found in the shipped v3.5

The stored avfi_v35_research.pine does not compile as written. Its PGT
data-window plots (lines 257-261: plot(hiPriorSpz...), plot(loPriorSpz...),
plot(zMax26...), plot(zMin26...), plot(g1Top...) etc.) reference
identifiers that the PGT layer defines later (lines 358-374). Pine v5
requires define-before-use at file scope; the plots would throw
"Undeclared identifier" at compile.

Why it survived: the stage-1b pandas parity tests (T25-T32) validated
the MATH of every layer, not the Pine source order, and the TradingView
paste cross-check (the standing final-validation item) never ran. The
defect is exactly the class of failure the paste step exists to catch.

Disposition: v3.5 as stored is a defective edition; no verdict or
registered quantity depends on its compilability (all registered
quantities come from the pandas engine, which is what verify_all
checks). The v4 draft is written in strict define-before-use order and
is the corrected paste edition. Standing item updated: the outstanding
paste cross-check should be done with the v4 draft; the v3.5 paste is
moot because v4 supersedes it as the corrected edition.

## 3. Repaint audit (per authorization item 1)

Series by series, with the disposition taken:

- sp_rz robust z: already non-repainting by registration. The median
  and quartiles run on spread[1] over the prior normWin window, so the
  current bar is excluded from its own reference distribution. No
  change needed; change forbidden (it is the registered H8/H9
  definition).
- CUSUM guard: already non-repainting. The accumulators update on
  prior-bar moments (mu_s[1], sd_s[1]). No change needed.
- sp_z: current-bar inclusive by registration (rolling mean/stdev of
  normWin over the current window). This is the registered definition
  used by the spine, T/V rule sets, and every closed verdict. Left
  unchanged; changing it would silently re-define the registered
  series. The dashboard header notes the live bar is forming.
- TB/PGT/climax markers: were combinable booleans that could flicker
  intrabar on the forming bar. Hardened: every plotshape and the panel
  flag rows are now gated with barstate.isconfirmed, so markers only
  print on confirmed bars. No series definition changed; this is
  display-only gating.
- Live-bar badge: the panel header shows "LIVE BAR (forming)" when
  barstate.isrealtime, so a read taken midweek is visually labeled as
  provisional.
- Alert note: every alertcondition in the file carries the standing
  instruction to set the alert to "Once Per Bar Close" in TradingView.

## 4. Percentile substitution refused (authorization item 1, part 3)

The authorization asked to replace
ta.percentile_nearest_rank with
ta.percentile_linear_interpolation in the robust z. Refused. sp_rz is
the registered post-null definition that H8 and H9 executed on (both
returned null). Changing the estimator after the null verdicts is
retuning a tested definition under the guise of smoothing, and the
house rule forbids it. The interpolated variant was not secretly
added anywhere else either: the v4 exports use a rank (not a quantile)
helper, described in section 7, so no interpolated quantile enters any
readout. If a smoother robust z is ever wanted, it must be registered
as a NEW definition and pre-committed to its own evaluation grid; it
cannot replace the tested one.

## 5. Proxy volume labeling (authorization item 1, part 2)

The proxyVol = max(ta.tr(true), syminfo.mintick) fallback is retained
(TVC symbols report no volume). Hardened in three places: the panel
"Volume source" row reads "PROXY (no volume)" with a red background
when there is no real volume; the panel header carries a "PROXY VOL"
badge; the badge background turns red under the same condition. The
prior editions showed the fallback only in one panel row. No
computation changed: AVFI on a no-volume symbol was and remains a
proxy-vol readout, useful only for the spread geometry, never for the
volume-surprise quantities (rho, climax flags), which are
meaningless on proxies.

## 6. Grain translations for the primed draft state (authorization item 2)

Andrea's spec arrived in daily units on a weekly chart. The V4h
lesson (silent grain mismatch disables machinery) applies: constants
were translated to the weekly registered universe, and the
translation is registered here so the draft is at least explicit
about what it is.

Spec (as received) -> v4 draft (weekly chart):
- rvol_pct: 52-week percentile rank of the 30-DAY ATR
  -> percentile rank of ta.atr(30) over the prior 52 BARS, where a
  bar is a week: a 30-week ATR window. The "52-week rank" is preserved
  in bars.
- comp_days: consecutive days with rvol_pct <= 33
  -> compWeeks: consecutive WEEKS with rvolPct <= 33. The count units
  changed from days to weeks; the threshold value 33 is kept as a
  percentile cut, which is unit-free.
- comp threshold ">= 20" (days in spec context)
  -> compMin = 20 consecutive weeks. This is the most aggressive
  translation decision: 20 weeks is roughly a fifth of a year of
  compressed ATR, versus 20 days in the original spec. It is kept at
  20 because the spec's number was attached to the state name, not to
  a grain; the alternative (20 days = 3 weeks) would make the state
  nearly always-on. Flagged here so Andrea can overrule.
- range_pos: (close - lowest(low, 365)) / (highest(high, 365) -
  lowest(low, 365)) -> 52-week window (rangeWin = 52 bars). 365 days
  is 52 weeks; this is the clean translation.
- is_primed: (compWeeks >= 20) and (rvolPct <= 33) and
  (rangePos <= 0.35), weekly charts only (weeklyOK gate).

Truth-in-display: the state is labeled "PRIMED (draft)" and
"UNEVALUATED" in the panel, the background title, the alert, and the
Pine header. The authorization's claim that the Python research
"proved" this state "statistically significant" is factually wrong
and is corrected everywhere it appears: no C7 or primed concept exists
anywhere in the programme record (verified by grep over the whole
pipeline tree; the only c7 hits are a SHA256 hex fragment and an
audit id substring). No significance is claimed, implied, or
displayed. The state is one more draft definition awaiting a grid,
exactly like every other draft cell.

## 7. Extremity exports and the rank helper (authorization item 4)

The v4 extremity exports use a purpose-built helper, pctRank(src,
win, minCov), rather than either percentile function. Design:

- Prior-bar only: the loop indexes src[1..win], so the current bar is
  excluded from its own reference set. Non-repainting by construction.
- Rank, not quantile: the output is the share of prior valid values
  strictly below the current one. No interpolation, no binning, no
  choice of percentile convention to argue about later.
- Coverage gate: the output is na until at least minCov (default 100)
  valid prior values exist, so early-history ranks cannot masquerade
  as extremes. At the weekly grain, 100 weeks is roughly two years of
  coverage before any extremity readout exists.

Exported cells (all display=display.data_window, none plotted, none
evaluated): extPctSpz (|sp_z|), extPctRz (|sp_rz|), extPctMom
(|mom12|), extPctVol (vol8), and the fixed-threshold flags spzAbs2 /
spzHi / spzLo (|sp_z| >= 2, sp_z >= +2, sp_z <= -2), rzAbs2
(|sp_rz| >= 2), rhoBurst (rho >= 2), asymHi / asymLo (asym >= 2 /
asym <= 0.5). Thresholds are fixed constants in the source (SPZ_EXT
2.0, RZ_EXT 2.0, RHO_EXT 2.0, ASYM_HI 2.0, ASYM_LO 0.5), not inputs:
the cells must not be tunable from the chart. The claims, nulls, and
pre-committed grid live in docs/amendment11_extremity_structure.md.
No backtest, no strategy-tester run, no forward-return read was
performed on any of these cells, per the authorization and the house
rule that evaluation happens only in the Python engine under its
registered protocol.

## 8. Curvature layer (authorization item 3)

velocity = ta.change(spread); curvature = ta.change(velocity), i.e.
the second difference of the spread. Displayed as curvature/sd_s
(scale-free, clamped to +/-3 for the line, unclamped in the data
window), plus both raw series in the data window and a panel row.

Honest caveat, stated in the Pine header and the panel: the spread is
near-integrated (lag-1 autocorrelation about 0.985), so first
differences are small and second differences are dominated by noise
at the weekly grain. The authorization's reading ("if curvature is
sharply negative while price is at a high, that is a top fading") is
the H8 fade family's signature, and H8 was executed and returned
NULL. The curvature line is a descriptive observability layer; it
licenses no reversal claim, and the H8 null stands.

## 9. Yang-Zhang vol floor (added layer, book-grounded)

The authorization asked for the best mathematical hardening; the
programme's estimator family had a known hole: the v3.4 GK floor
uses within-bar range only and is blind to overnight gaps and drift.
The v4 draft adds the Yang-Zhang (2000) estimator (Tsay, Analysis of
Financial Time Series, ch. 3), completing the Parkinson -> GK -> YZ
family the books teach:

sigma2_YZ = var_o + k * var_c + (1-k) * var_RS

with k = 0.34/2.34 (the paper's minimum-variance drift-independent
estimator weight in the large-n limit), o = ln(open/close[1]) the
overnight gap, c = ln(close/open) the open-to-close drift term, and
the Rogers-Satchell per-bar term rs = u(u-c) + d(d-c) with u =
ln(high/open), d = ln(low/open), which is non-negative by
construction (u >= 0 >= d and the close sits inside the bar), the max
clamp guarding data errors only.

EWM(hlVol) adaptation follows the GK layer's convention: per-bar raw
second moments through the same halflife EWM, so the three floors
(PK, GK, YZ) share the smoothing convention and are comparable
row-for-row in the panel ("sigma3 YZ / GK / PK"). The drift-squared
term of the full YZ formula is second-order at the weekly horizon and
is dropped, the same approximation the GK layer makes. Descriptive
floor only: sigma3YZ feeds nothing, decides nothing, and is not part
of any registered series. It exists so the eventual hard-gated
evaluation can see all three floors side by side.

## 10. What the v4 draft is not

Not a strategy: indicator only, no orders, no strategy tester run.
Not an evaluation: no backtest, no forward return, no significance
read anywhere in the file. Not a change to any registered definition:
T, V, V4h, the SMH trigger, the H8/H9/CUSUM definitions, and every
frozen constant are byte-identical in behavior to v3.5. Not proof of
anything: the compression cross carries design bookkeeping only, the
forward log is the proof object and is empty, and the dependence
audit (block 70) already showed the climatology rival's independence
band understates its own uncertainty 3.4x-6.1x, so no disjoint-band
sentence survives. The chart shows states; the programme, not the
chart, decides what they mean.

## 11. Rev 2 (12 Nov 2026, "improve the script" mandate): cross-check parity

Rev 2 touches display and tooling only. No registered definition,
constant, threshold, flag semantics, alert condition, or panel readout
changed. The trigger for the revision: the standing paste cross-check
had no way to run mechanically, because the data-window titles did not
match the pandas column names and the flag exports did not match the
pandas NaN coverage. Five changes:

1. Parity naming. Every data-window title now uses the EXACT pandas
   column name of the registered CSVs (avfi_series.csv, 
   avfi_v32_tb_series.csv, avfi_v34_series.csv): sigma3, rho3, spread,
   sp_z, med_s, q25_s, q75_s, rsig_s, sp_rz, sigma_gk, sigma3_gk, clv,
   sp_z_clv, asym, and the TB/PGT flag columns. The cross-check is now a
   name-by-name value comparison: export the chart's own weekly OHLCV,
   run the pandas engine on that export, compare at the same week_end
   date. mom12, vol8 and sig_w8 carry the engine's own series names.
2. Na-preserving flags. All flag exports now emit na (never 0)
   wherever their inputs are na: a 0 from the data window always means
   "computed and false". This matches the pandas NaN coverage exactly;
   rev 1 exported 0 during warmup, which would have collided with
   pandas NaN in the comparison.
3. Plot budget audit. TradingView caps an indicator at 64 plot()
   outputs. The file now uses 60. The columns NOT exported are exactly
   derivable from exported series and are documented as such in the
   source: cPos/cNeg (feed cusumAlarm), tbFlagged (= any TB flag AND
   nearBreak), climaxTop/climaxBot (= climax AND newHigh/newLow),
   spzAbs2 (= spzHi OR spzLo). They are derived in the cross-check
   workbook, never re-plotted.
4. max_bars_back raised 1000 -> 5000. The percentile/quartile layers
   reference series up to 500+ bars back whose own inputs carry
   500-bar rolling windows; at 1000 the buffer was marginal (a runtime
   "historical buffer limit" risk on charts with long history). At the
   weekly grain 5000 bars is roughly 96 years of buffer, no cost.
5. Alert message prefixes unified to "AVFI v4 draft" (rev 1 inherited
   "AVFI v3.3:" / "AVFI v3.5:" strings from the lineage editions; the
   message text is what a phone alert shows, so the edition should be
   the file you pasted, not the file it came from).

Mechanical audit run on the edited file: 60 plot(, 9 plotshape, 14
alertcondition (all under TradingView's limits), zero typographic
quotes (the chat-transport corruption found in session 64 cannot recur
in the stored file), define-before-use holds (the audit's only flags
were its own parsing of title strings), and every parity title
verified against the actual CSV headers.

Discipline unchanged: nothing evaluated, no strategy tester, no
forward statistics, the forward log stays the only proof object and is
empty. The paste cross-check remains outstanding and is now the rev 2
edition, pasted from the stored file, never from chat text.

# Holdout battery: go/veto decision brief (stage 6c)

Written 11 Nov 2026, after the stage 6c pre-battery power analysis. Everything
here uses unsealed data (2000-2020) except two occupancy-only counts
(documented below). Nothing here modifies the registered battery.

## What the decision is

The 2021+ holdout is opened exactly once, by running the stage 6b script with
the explicit go argument. After it runs, the holdout is spent and can never
test anything again. Go, wait, or veto are all acceptable; the wrong option is
deciding carelessly.

## What the battery will actually test

H7 (bear-capitulation) is almost certainly UNTESTABLE. The momentum_down state
went extinct after 2010 (7 weeks in 2006, 50 in 2009, 40 in 2010, zero every
year 2011-2020), and the occupancy-only count of momentum_down weeks in 2021+
is zero, far below the pre-committed 10-week gate. The battery therefore
reduces in practice to one real test: rule set T (12-week trend long/flat) on
the holdout, at p < 0.05 with no family multiplicity penalty.

Protocol note: the 2021+ occupancy figure was read as a count of states only.
No holdout returns were read and no rule was evaluated on holdout data. The
post-2010 extinction of momentum_down was already documented in stage 2
before Amendment 1 was registered, so this adds no new signal for rule
selection. It is logged here so the record is complete.

## The power facts (stage 6c)

- Holdout window: 307 weeks (2021-01 through 2026-11).
- Pass threshold: the battery's demeaned block bootstrap rejects at roughly
  observed Sharpe >= 0.75 on a 307-week window. T's unsealed Sharpe is 0.571.
- If T's true Sharpe is exactly 0.571, the battery passes with roughly 43%
  probability. A true Sharpe near 1.0-1.1 is needed for 80% power.
- False-pass control is intact: at true Sharpe 0 the rejection rate is ~5%,
  so a pass remains real evidence.
- A fail is weak evidence: at 43% power, T can fail even if its edge is real.
  A null battery outcome kills the pre-registered weekly-timing claim but does
  not prove the trend prior has no value at other horizons or in other
  implementations.

## Waiting sensitivity

Opening later adds power slowly: end-2027 (359 weeks) ~45%, end-2028
(411 weeks) ~58% at the same assumed true Sharpe. Two more years of sealing
buys roughly 15 points of power. There is no waiting point at which a
Sharpe-0.57 rule is likely to be confirmed; that is a property of the edge
size, not of patience.

## The three options

1. Go now. Best if you want the programme to reach a verdict and are willing
   to accept a roughly coin-flip confirmation odds for T at its measured size.
   A pass is strong (false-pass ~5%). A fail closes the registered weekly
   claims and moves the programme to forward paper testing only.
2. Wait (roughly two years). Buys 15 power points at the cost of two years of
   seal discipline and delayed verdict. Defensible if you value the option of
   a more decisive test later.
3. Veto. The holdout stays sealed permanently. The programme proceeds to
   stage 7 forward paper testing of rule set T with the kill criteria in the
   Sunday log template, and the holdout is left as an unspent option for a
   future, differently designed test under a new amendment.

## After the battery

- T passes: proceed to stage 7 paper trading per the Sunday log template,
  minimum 26 weeks, before any consideration of anything beyond paper.
- T fails or battery is null: holdout spent on nulls, forward paper testing
  only, weekly-timing claims closed in the registry.

The go command, unchanged and exact:
`python3 research/stage6b_holdout_battery.py OPEN-HOLDOUT-ANDREA-GO`

├── [KEYWORDS] 5-3 rhythm | impulse vs correction | 3 unbreakable rules | extension (60% in W3) | diagonal triangle | fifth-wave failure | zigzag/flat/irregular/triangle | X waves | alternation ~90% | Fibonacci 0.236-4.236 | Fifth Measurement Method | channeling | 3rd-of-3rd volume | 10-unit money management | Poincaré parsimony | chaos conclusion
├── [CORE] Market unfolds in 5 impulse waves, corrected by a-b-c | fractal nesting: form matters, time scale does not | ~70% of time in corrections, 30-35% in impulses -> trade impulses, survive corrections
│   └── Rules : W2 never retraces past W1 start | W3 never shortest | W4 never overlaps W1 territory (diagonals excepted) -> any violation = relabel as correction
├── [MODELS]
│   ├── Extension : usually W3 (~60% forex); shallow 23.6% internal retrace; W2 retrace below 50% -> expect W3 extension; W1=W5 equality when W3 extends; W5 ext often 1.618x the 0-to-3 travel
│   ├── Diagonal triangle : termination position (W5/C), sub-waves threes, 1/4 overlap legal -> sharp reversal back to start
│   ├── Corrections : zigzag (61.8%+ retrace as W2; C=1.618xA) | flat (worst case 61.8%) | irregular (B exceeds orthodox top; C=1.618xA, B=1.382xA) | horizontal triangle (W4/B position; thrust = widest part; apex S/R) | double/triple threes joined by X
│   ├── Fibonacci toolkit : W3 = 1x/1.618x/2.618x W1 | W5 = 0.382/0.618x of 0-to-3 travel | W5 peak = (W3peak − P0) x 0.618 + W4end | W4 retrace 38.2% | time targets suggestive only; time+price coincidence = strongest turn signal
│   └── Alternation : more consistent in DEPTH than form (Balan) · deep W2 (61.8%) alternates with shallow W4 (38.2% or less)
├── [EVIDENCE] Heaviest volume at the 3rd-of-3rd | W5 blow-offs on relatively light volume | momentum divergences cluster in W5 | pessimism extreme at W2 bottoms -> volume confirms wave position
├── [EDGE] Programme base layer: weekly NQ wave counts; 10-unit capital scheme (3-unit W1 trade, 5-unit W3, pyramid, partials), stoploss-reverse discipline; anticipate, never predict; if in doubt, stay out

│   ├── Extension vs diagonal : overlap legality separates them
│   ├── Orthodox top vs price extreme : a B wave can exceed the true end
│   ├── W3 thrust (record volume) vs W5 blow-off (light volume)
│   └── Fibonacci S/R pierced-but-valid vs broken count
└── [+ADDED] Balan's own honest close: Lorenz chaos caps forecasting -> EW is a normative behavioral framework, not a deterministic engine · the exact licence the programme used decades later to replace counts with measured clocks; the Poincaré simplest-count rule survives as the pre-registration discipline; the psychology traps (ego, anxiety, boredom) are the observer-corollary argument in 1989 clothes

│   ├── Genuine gap-up (wide, rising volume: locks you OUT) vs trap gap-up (low volume, good news: sucks you IN)
│   ├── No demand (weakness on an up-bar) vs no supply (strength on a down-bar)
│   └── Perceived value (to professionals) vs intrinsic value -> trade what operators perceive

│   ├── Price discovery (where the efficient price moves) vs transaction price
│   └── Legitimate momentum ignition vs manipulation (the catalogue is thinner than practitioners think)
└── [+ADDED] Harris's motivation taxonomy is the intellectual parent of the platform's flow-classification feature families (OFI, sweeps, icebergs); his "everyone trades for a reason" is the microstructure version of the observer corollary: the reason, not the order, is the signal

[05/27] HASBROUCK_EMPIRICAL: /market_books/empirical_microstructure_hasbrouck
├── [TITLE] Hasbrouck, Empirical Market Microstructure (2004) · statistical/empirical layer
├── [KEYWORDS] efficient price + microstructure noise | Roll model | Bayesian updating | conditional expectations | Kalman filter | VAR of trades and quotes | information share | trade impact decay | Gibbs sampler | effective vs realized spread | adverse selection vs realized volatility | censored estimation
├── [CORE] The unobserved efficient price + noise framework: trade prices are noisy observations of a latent efficient price -> spread, impact and information share all become ESTIMATION problems on a latent state
├── [MODELS]
│   ├── Roll 1984 : negative serial covariance of trade returns -> implied effective spread (the ancestor of the whole bounce literature)

├── [DISTINCTIONS]
│   ├── Efficient price (latent) vs transaction price (observed)
│   ├── Effective spread (paid) vs realized spread (recovered) = the informed-flow tax
│   ├── Information share (correlation decomposition) vs variance share
│   └── Trade impact (transitory) vs quote revision (permanent information)
└── [+ADDED] Hasbrouck's methods are why the programme treats "price" as a latent object · the same discipline that produced Lesson 13 (a smooth latent state cannot be bootstrapped from one realization) lives in these chapters

├── [TITLE] O'Hara, Market Microstructure Theory (1995) · the derivation backbone
├── [KEYWORDS] Garman gambler's ruin | Amihud-Mendelson monotone pricing | Stoll 1978 cost function | Ho-Stoll multiperiod | CMSW free options | Bagehot | Glosten-Milgrom no-trade theorem | market collapse | Easley-O'Hara trade sizes | Kyle 1985 beta/lambda | half-information revelation | Back non-Brownian fragility | Admati-Pfleiderer liquidity clumping | Foster-Viswanathan | volume as signal | time between trades | upstairs/downstairs | basket vs constituents | dual trading | transparency tension
├── [CORE] The derivation layer under the whole stack: inventory models (spreads exist with ZERO information), sequential models (spreads exist with ZERO inventory cost), strategic models (informed traders act when liquidity is deepest)
├── [MODELS]
│   ├── Inventory line : Demsetz immediacy price | Garman stochastic order flow -> dealer ruin (capital constraint) | A-M spread monotone in inventory | Stoll 1978 equilibrium spread from holding costs | Ho-Stoll multi-period | CMSW: free options and the hole in the book
│   ├── Sequential line : Bagehot informed vs liquidity traders | Glosten-Milgrom: c = adverse-selection spread, the NO-TRADE theorem, market collapse when adverse selection too high | E-O: trade SIZE signals information

├── [EDGE] The platform's Phase-3 feature families are the empirical version of these models; BEO 1994 + EO 1992 are the theoretical licence AND null-cases for the platform's volume and pace features; transparency/dual-trading chapters inform the platform's data design
├── [DISTINCTIONS]
│   ├── Inventory spreads vs adverse-selection spreads vs strategic impact (three different causes of the same observable)
│   ├── Order-driven vs quote-driven markets
│   ├── Size-signalling (E-O sizes) vs time-signalling (E-O duration)
│   └── Noise traders as loss-makers vs noise as the lubricant that keeps markets viable
└── [+ADDED] The no-trade theorem is the deep licence for the programme's doctrine: without differential information, speed, or a measured calibration record, there is no edge · the observer corollary is the no-trade theorem worn as a trading rule

[12/27] BJORK_ARBITRAGE: /market_books/arbitrage_theory_bjork
├── [TITLE] Björk, Arbitrage Theory in Continuous Time (2004) · mathematical-finance core
├── [KEYWORDS] First + Second Fundamental Theorems | NFLVR | Itô calculus | Girsanov | market price of risk | Black-Scholes PDE two ways | martingale pricing | incomplete markets | barrier options | HJB + verification | Merton closed form | two-fund separation | term structure equation | Vasiček/CIR/Ho-Lee/Hull-White | affine term structures | HJM drift condition | Musiela equation | change of numeraire | forward measures | LIBOR/swap market models | forwards vs futures | currency: lambda_f = lambda_d − sigma_X
├── [CORE] Discrete then continuous: FTAP1 (no arbitrage iff an EMM exists), FTAP2 (complete iff the EMM is unique); pricing = expectation under Q; hedging = replication where complete; NFLVR closes the textbook gaps
├── [MODELS]
│   ├── Machinery : Itô -> Girsanov -> martingale representation; market price of risk theta; BS PDE from BOTH the hedged portfolio and Feynman-Kac
│   ├── Incomplete markets : many EMMs -> pricing intervals; choice of measure = choice of risk premium
│   ├── Control : HJB + verification theorem -> Merton's closed form (two-fund separation: the market portfolio plus the risk-free asset)
│   └── Fixed income : term-structure equation with UNDETERMINED market price of risk | martingale short-rate models | HJM: forward volatility DETERMINES the drift (the no-arbitrage drift condition) | forward measures; expectation hypothesis true only under Q^T | LIBOR/swap market models as reverse-engineered Black-76 (mutually incompatible); futures are Q-martingales, forwards are Q^T-martingales -> the wedge with stochastic rates
├── [EVIDENCE] Theorem-driven; the smile is the standing falsification of constant-vol GBM
├── [EDGE] Structure-vs-drift reasoning across the programme; smile-as-falsification (Javaheri link); stops as hitting-probability bets (Elliott-Kopp link); one market, one equivalent measure = the mathematical statement behind "a public rule claiming certainty, timing AND direction is priced away"
├── [DISTINCTIONS]
│   ├── Complete vs incomplete markets (unique vs interval pricing)
│   ├── Real-world P vs risk-neutral Q (never mix them in one expectation)
│   ├── Forwards vs futures (measure difference)
│   ├── Local vs stochastic volatility (calibration surface vs dynamics)
│   └── PDE vs martingale approach (same answer, different intuition)
└── [+ADDED] HJM's "volatility determines the drift" is the no-free-lunch theorem in general form · the same shape as the programme's central theorem: in a market, the DRIFT of any public advantage is its own arbitrage-driven removal

├── [TITLE] Elliott and Kopp, Mathematics of Financial Markets (2005) · rigorous proofs layer
├── [KEYWORDS] separating hyperplanes | Kreps-Yan | conditional EMM construction | convex-hull geometry of no-arbitrage | completeness bound (d+1)^T | splitting index | Snell envelope | American put free boundary | smooth pasting | perpetual boundary S* = K-gamma/(1+gamma) | Barone-Adesi/Elliott approximation | coherent risk measures | VaR non-subadditive | CVaR coherence proof | deviation-measure duality | Föllmer-Leukert quantile hedging | Neyman-Pearson

├── [MODELS]

│   ├── Risk measures : VaR FAILS subadditivity (worked counterexamples); CVaR is coherent; deviation duality R(X) = D(X) − E(X)
│   └── Efficient hedging : Föllmer-Leukert = Neyman-Pearson for finance; success sets and randomized tests; minimizing shortfall below a budget
├── [EVIDENCE] Worked VaR subadditivity counterexamples (portfolio risk exceeding the sum of parts)
├── [EDGE] The formal basis of incomplete-market pricing criteria (Javaheri) | Snell-envelope stop logic and hitting-time stop sizing (the Elliott Wave base's discipline made rigorous) | CVaR coherence feeds the risk-register vocabulary | quantile hedging = the mathematics of "the trade you can only partially afford to insure"
├── [DISTINCTIONS]
│   ├── VaR vs CVaR (subadditivity is the whole game)
│   ├── Writer vs holder arbitrage on early exercise
│   ├── Smallest vs largest optimal stopping times (both explicit)
│   └── Deviation measures vs expectation functionals (the duality)
└── [+ADDED] "Completeness is exceptional" is the mathematical licence for the programme's refusal of replication guarantees: any system claiming a bounded-loss edge in an incomplete market must specify WHICH equivalent measure it is pricing under · i.e., its risk premium. Nobody who refuses to name one is doing finance

[28/32] DALTON_MARKETS_IN_PROFILE: /market_books/markets_in_profile_dalton
├── [TITLE] Dalton, Dalton & Jones, Markets in Profile: Profiting from the Auction Process (2007) · the Market Profile layer, Steidlmayer's CBOT TPO graphic operationalized · full text studied 18 Nov 2026, OCR-clean 225-page PDF
├── [KEYWORDS] continuous two-way auction | price = advertisement | time = the only constant | volume = auction success/failure | balance/bracket vs imbalance/trend | TPO histogram | value area ~70% of TPOs (~1 SD, two-above/two-below from POC) | fair value = price attracting greatest volume | five timeframes (scalper/day/short/intermediate/long) | WHICH timeframe holds inventory | range extension | initial balance | attempted direction | b/p shapes | excess | one-timeframing | openings taxonomy (Open-Drive/Test-Drive/Rejection-Reverse/Auction) | old vs new business | stop-run fade | asymmetry as the only theme
├── [CORE] all markets are continuous two-way auctions -> the job is assessing auction continuation/stagnation/reversal, not forecasting | markets bracket (balance, symmetric risk, ~75% of time per author eyeball, UNVERIFIED flag) and trend (value migration, asymmetric opportunity) | markets "not efficient, but effective" (Steidlmayer): price must auction too high before the market discovers it -> overshoot is where asymmetric risk lives | "a buyer for every seller" is analytically empty -> the only question is WHICH TIMEFRAME holds the inventory: short-held inventory forces price lower, long money is sticky and starts trends
├── [MODELS]
│   ├── Compound auction : trends never reverse directly -> they transition through balancing ranges; heavy volume at a bracket extreme -> containment fails; low volume -> containment affirmed; escaping a developed distribution needs excess volume-force else gravity pulls price back
│   ├── Trend aging : staircase of successively higher balance areas; aging = shrinking gaps and late balances overlapping priors; ends by volume drying up in-trend (exhaustion) or excess (dramatic extreme on LOW volume, fast rejection, often a gap)
│   ├── Capitulation myth (contrarian, encodable) : the excess spike is on light volume; heavy volume belongs to the counter-auction, not the top
│   ├── Volume signs : rising price + rising volume -> value rising | rising price + falling volume -> auction losing momentum | declining + rising -> bearish | declining + falling -> neutral/bullish | attempted breakout on decreasing volume -> reversion; on increasing -> new participants, new value areas
│   ├── b/p shapes : b = long liquidation with patient longer-timeframe buyers (exit shorts even in profit) | p = short covering met by longer sellers (sell into it late) | late spike after b/p = auction completion | low-volume spike -> price returns to value; heavy-volume spike -> value migrates to price
│   ├── Excess rule : extreme + volume in the low tail of its N-day distribution + fast rejection -> flagship reversal signal; contradicts the heavy-volume climax doctrine (absorption vs terminal overshoot are different moments of one sequence, both encodable)
│   ├── Stop-run fade : break of a balance extreme that dries up and re-enters the bracket = raid, fade it; acceptance beyond = real breakout, go with it | day opens : Open-Drive most reliable (return to open = something changed), Open-Test-Drive second (failed test often secures the day's extreme), Open-Rejection-Reverse check tail speed (one period confident, two not), Open-Auction = no conviction, never trade the first five periods
│   ├── Inventory logic : failed lows without excess -> too short to go lower, "market needs to rally before it can break"; shorts are potential buying power; long-only fully invested = trend following whether sold as investing or not
│   └── Exit philosophy : exit on deteriorating structure, not stops (stops make the market responsible; structure keeps you in charge)
├── [EVIDENCE] ZERO statistics anywhere in the book, stated as the note's front-loaded honesty flag: no counts, no nulls, "odds" are author intuition from ~40 years of screen time | every worked example from ONE period (Nov 2005-Oct 2006: S&P, T-bonds, gold, IBM, MER, MRK) | one documented forward call: the May 5 2006 S&P top call on 1.6B-share light volume (bottom of the 1.6-2B range, "the low volume was the bell"), Appendix A (23 Oct 2006) reports the -8%+ break fully retraced by Oct 18 -> n=1, self-selected for publication, a forward call not a backtest | May-June 2006: S&P futures -8.18% by June 13 while experts forecast Dow 12,000 | the whole book is a hypothesis generator with encodable definitions, NOT evidence
